Existing methods fall short: task-specific stage-aware models are accurate but require per-task annotations, while general vision-language-model (VLM) reward models are broadly applicable but too coarse for fine-grained long-horizon progress. We introduce RM, a multi-task stage-aware reward model that combines an action-primitive-based stage estimator with a multi-gate Mixture-of-Experts (MMoE) value head to produce dense per-step rewards across manipulation tasks. Building on RM, we further propose SPIRAL (Self-Policy Improvement via Reward-Aligned Learning), an on-policy reward-guided framework that improves VLA policies from cheap autonomous rollouts. On a 10-task benchmark, RM reduces value-estimation MSE by 80% over the strongest baselines; when used in SPIRAL, it improves task success from around 50% to near-perfect performance on Folding Shorts (58% to 100%) and Cleaning Whiteboard (50% to 90%), showing that high-quality dense rewards are key to a stable robot data flywheel. Project website: https://qianzhong-chen.github.io/sarm2.github.io/.

</details>


### [110] [OMG: Omni-Modal Motion Generation for Generalist Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10340)
*Siqiao Huang,Kun-Ying Lee,Dongming Qiao,Guanqi He,Zhenyu Wang,Yitang Li,Shaoting Zhu,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OMG提出了一种用于人形机器人全身控制的模块化框架，包含一个可扩展的"大脑"模块（基于扩散模型，支持语言、音频和参考动作等多模态条件）和一个反应式运动跟踪"小脑"。通过精细的数据整理、过滤和标注流程，实现了从多样条件到全身运动的高质量生成，在多项实验中展现出SOTA性能和良好的模型扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形全身控制方法要么依赖繁重的奖励工程设计且只能处理少数技能策略，要么是难以扩展到新输入模态的运动跟踪器。作者认为关键在于构建一个可扩展的"大脑"，能够推理多种条件模态，并建立在反应式运动跟踪"小脑"之上，以模仿生物运动系统的分层结构。

Method: 1) 精细的数据整理、过滤和标注流程来获取高质量多模态数据；2) 基于扩散模型的运动生成骨干网络，支持语言、音频和人类参考动作等组合式、可扩展的多模态条件输入。

Result: OMG在全身控制任务上取得SOTA性能，展现出良好的模型扩展行为，并能高效适应新分布和新模态。

Conclusion: 该工作为人形机器人基础模型迈出了具体一步，证明了面向通用人形控制的omni-modal全身控制器的可行性。

Abstract: Humanoid whole-body control has made significant progress in recent years, yet existing approaches remain limited to few-skill policies with heavy reward engineering, or motion trackers that are difficult to extend to new input modalities. We argue that the key to general-purpose humanoid control is to build a scalable brain, a module capable of reasoning with diverse conditioning modalities, atop a reactive motion tracking cerebellum, mirroring the hierarchical structure of biological motor systems. Two challenges arise in realizing this vision: acquiring a vast amount of high-quality data to achieve general purpose control, and equipping the generator with the capability to condition on compositional, extensible multi-modal inputs. We present OMG, which addresses these challenges with a meticulous data curation, filtering and labeling pipeline, as well as a diffusion-based motion generation backbone that conditions on language, audio, and human reference motions. Extensive experiments validate OMG as an omni-modal whole-body controller exhibiting state-of-the-art performance, model scaling behavior and efficient adaptation to new distributions and modalities, marking a concrete step toward foundation models for humanoid robots.

</details>


### [111] [Rethinking Embodied Navigation via Relational Inductive Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10348)
*Weitao An,Chenghao Xu,Xu Yang,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DB-Nav通过双重关系偏置（激活偏置与抑制偏置）重塑搜索空间，利用在线观测和失败访问修正前沿探索值，在无需昂贵在线VLM推理的情况下显著提升ObjectNav的成功率和SPL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇检测器/VLM在物体导航中忽略"什么不可信"——即语义线索中的系统性误导证据（假阳性、过时静态先验、缺乏具身验证导致的重复失败探索），这些问题污染了地图构建和决策，且根植于真实场景的结构化物体关系。

Method: 提出DB-Nav框架，将目标中心关系分解为激活偏置（传播上下文证据）和抑制偏置（通过感知混淆和动作级证伪抑制不可靠区域），统一为关系激活-抑制探索图（RAI-Exploration Graph），利用在线观测和失败访问调制前沿探索值。

Result: 在ObjectNav基准上显著优于现有方法，在成功率和SPL指标上均取得领先。

Conclusion: DB-Nav提供了一种轻量级、可解释且鲁棒的导航框架，无需昂贵的在线VLM推理，通过结构性关系偏置有效解决了开放词汇感知中的不可靠语义线索问题。

Abstract: Object navigation requires an agent to locate a target in an unknown environment through visual observations. Existing methods typically rely on open-vocabulary detectors or vision-language models (VLMs) to answer where to search, but often overlook what not to trust - which semantic cues are unreliable. Open-vocabulary perception is prone to systematic misleading evidence: false positives, outdated static priors, and repeated failed exploration due to lack of embodied verification, which contaminates mapping and decision-making. Such errors are rooted in structured object relations in real-world scenes. To address this, we propose DB-Nav, a framework that reshapes the search space via dual relational biases. It factorizes target-centric relations into an Activation Bias (propagates contextual evidence) and an Inhibition Bias (suppresses unreliable regions via perceptual confusion and action-level falsification). These biases are unified into a Relational Activation-Inhibition Exploration Graph that modulates frontier exploration values using online observations and failed accesses. Experiments on ObjectNav benchmarks show that DB-Nav significantly outperforms existing methods in success rate (SR) and Success weighted by Path Length (SPL), offering a lightweight, interpretable, and robust navigation framework without costly online VLM reasoning.

</details>


### [112] [HiMem-WAM: Hierarchical Memory-Gated World Action Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10363)
*Xiaoquan Sun,Ruijian Zhang,Chen Cao,Yihan Sun,Jiahui Chen,Zetian Xu,Bo Chen,Haijier Chen,Zhen Yang,Jiarun Zhu,Yijun Hong,JingZhe Xu,Jingrui Pang,Mingqi Yuan,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiMem-WAM 提出了一种分层记忆门控的世界动作模型，通过分层潜在动作框架（低层运动+高层技能潜在变量）和边界感知记忆门，在长程机器人操作任务中实现了更好的任务相关记忆和因果推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型在长程机器人操作中缺乏任务相关的记忆能力，难以处理需要长期依赖的复杂操作任务。

Method: 1) 层级化潜在动作框架：联合学习低层运动潜在变量和高层技能潜在变量，提供结构化时间抽象；2) 边界感知记忆门：在预测的技能转换边界写入紧凑任务状态，实现无需测试时生成未来视频或光流估计的因果推理。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-PLUS、RMBench 及真实世界任务上评估，层级化潜在变量提升了部署扰动的鲁棒性，记忆模块显著提升了依赖记忆的长程操作性能。

Conclusion: 分层记忆门控机制有效解决了WAM在长程操作中的任务记忆问题，层级化潜在空间同时提升了鲁棒性和因果推理能力。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a new powerful paradigm for embodied intelligence, learning action-relevant visual dynamics that significantly enhance generalization and robustness. However, existing WAMs still struggle with task-relevant memory in long-horizon robotic manipulation. To address this, we present HiMem-WAM, a Hierarchical Memory-Gated WAM that integrates motion-centric latent actions, high-level skill latents, and boundary-triggered memory updates. Specifically, we develop a hierarchical latent action framework that jointly learns low-level motion and high-level skill latents, providing structured temporal abstraction. Meanwhile, a boundary-aware memory gate writes compact task states at predicted skill transitions, enabling causal inference without test-time generation of future video or optical flow estimation. Evaluated on LIBERO, LIBERO-PLUS, RMBench and real-world tasks, HiMem-WAM shows that hierarchical latents improve robustness under deployment perturbations, and the memory module substantially benefits memory-dependent long-horizon manipulation.

</details>


### [113] [A Practical Recipe Towards Improving Sim-and-Real Correlation for VLA Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10366)
*Shuo Wang,Hanyuan Xu,Yingdong Hu,Fanqi Lin,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了仿真环境在视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略评估中的"仿真-真实相关性"问题，通过跨多个仿真平台、VLA策略、任务和扰动因素的系统实验，衡量仿真评估能否保持真实世界的结论一致性、性能相关性和失败模式匹配，并探索如何利用仿真进行策略改进，为仿真平台设计者和策略开发者提供了统一的分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管仿真已成为VLA策略评估的重要工具，但现有仿真基准尚未被广泛接受为真实世界策略评估的可靠替代。缺乏对仿真与真实世界之间相关性的系统理解和量化分析。

Method: 在多个仿真平台上，针对多种VLA策略、任务和扰动因素，系统测量策略排名一致性、性能相关性以及扰动层面的失败模式匹配度，并分析仿真微调的效果和数据量对仿真-真实对齐的影响。

Result: 揭示了现有仿真器的局限性，识别了哪些仿真信号与真实部署更对齐，并给出了仿真微调何时有益以及后训练数据量如何影响仿真-真实对齐的具体结论。

Conclusion: 提供了一个统一的框架来测量、解释和提升仿真对VLA策略的有用性，为仿真设计者和使用仿真进行策略开发的实践者提供指导。

Abstract: Simulation has become an essential tool for evaluating and improving vision-language-action (VLA) policies, offering scalable, reproducible, and controllable alternatives to costly real-world robot evaluation. Recent simulation benchmarks have made substantial progress on realism and diversity, yet these platforms have not been widely adopted as reliable proxies for real-world policy evaluation. In this work, we investigate this issue through the lens of sim-and-real correlation. We conduct a systematic study across multiple simulation platforms, VLA policies, tasks, and perturbation factors, measuring whether simulated evaluation preserves real-world conclusions in terms of policy ranking consistency, performance correlation, and perturbation-wise failure patterns. This analysis allows us to characterize the limitations of existing simulators and identify what kinds of simulation signals are more aligned with real-world deployment. We further examine how users should exploit simulation for policy improvement, including when simulator-based finetuning is beneficial and how the amount of post-training data affects sim-and-real alignment. Overall, our work provides a unified framework for measuring, interpreting, and improving the usefulness of simulation for VLA policies, offering guidance both for simulator designers and for practitioners who use simulation as part of the policy development pipeline.

</details>


### [114] [Test-time Adversarial Takeover: A Real-time Hijacking Interface against Robotic Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10371)
*Zi Yin,Peilin Chai,Siyuan Huang,Zhanhao Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种测试时对抗性接管（TAKO）攻击方法，通过可重复使用的通用补丁干扰视觉条件，将冻结的机器人策略变为远程操控工具，在多个任务和模型上实现100%接管成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗攻击主要破坏任务成功或引发异常行为，缺乏对更强威胁（实时接管机器人策略）的研究。作者探索是否可以通过视觉扰动将冻结的机器人策略变为攻击者可操控的执行器。

Method: 通过可微扩散推理学习少量可重复使用的通用对抗补丁（称为"词汇"），测试时在相机流中切换这些补丁以组合攻击者选择的轨迹。扰动作用于视觉条件路径，诱导的偏差能通过迭代生成推理持续存在。

Result: 在4个任务（2D操控、模拟空中投递、模拟地面导航、物理世界地面导航）、两种视觉编码器、三种生成推理族（DDPM、DDIM、流匹配）上，人类操作员在每个评估设置中实现100%接管成功率。表明目标-策略匹配这一自然基线因无法可靠地在分布外目标偏移上自我监督而失败。

Conclusion: 揭示了扩散式动作生成策略存在严重安全漏洞，视觉条件路径可被利用实现完全远程操控，且现有防御机制对此类攻击无效。

Abstract: Diffusion-based action generation has become a foundational component of embodied AI, but its reliance on visual conditioning leaves deployed visuomotor policies vulnerable to adversarial manipulation. Most prior attacks focus on disruption: they perturb the observation stream to reduce task success or induce erratic behavior. We study a stronger threat, Test-time Adversarial Takeover (TAKO), in which an attacker obtains a real-time steering interface over a frozen robot policy and turns it into a remotely piloted instrument. TAKO learns a small vocabulary of reusable universal patches through differentiable diffusion inference; at test time, the attacker switches among these patches in the camera stream to compose attacker-chosen trajectories. This works because the perturbation acts on the visual conditioning pathway, where the induced bias can persist through iterative generative inference. We further show that the natural targeted baseline, target-policy matching, fails because the victim policy cannot reliably supervise itself on out-of-distribution target shifts. Across four tasks (2D manipulation, simulated aerial delivery, simulated ground navigation, and physical-world ground navigation), two visual encoders (ResNet-18 and EfficientNet-B0 + Transformer), and three generative inference families (DDPM, DDIM, and flow matching), human operators achieve 100\% takeover success on attacker-defined objectives in every evaluated setting. The project page is available at https://tako-attack.github.io.

</details>


### [115] [UMI-Bench 1.0: An Open and Reproducible Real-World Benchmark for Tabletop Robotic Manipulation with UMI Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10382)
*Shi Jin,Yuntian Wang,Yuhui Duan,Di Wu,Gaoqi Dong,Xiaohang Liu,Xiaotong Li,Hongfei Jia,Zehao Zhang,Tianyu Wang,Zhongjie Jia,Yuanqi Yao,Chenjia Bai,Zhaxizhuoma,Siao Liu,Nieqing Cao,Jin Wang,Chao Yu,Yan Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UMI-Bench 1.0 是首个专为标准化评估 UMI 风格操作策略而设计的本地优先真实机器人基准测试，统一了数据收集、场景重置、策略执行、结果记录和任务因素分析的全流程协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真实机器人基准测试未针对 UMI 数据到部署的全链路设计，而 UMI 策略的性能高度依赖腕部视角、动作表示、数据收集和物理部署之间的耦合，因此需要一个专门、可复现的评估平台。

Method: 提出 UMI-Bench 1.0 协议，统一数据收集、场景重置、策略执行、结果记录和任务因素分析，实现完整的可复现和可审计评估流程。

Result: 建立了首个专用于 UMI 风格操作模型真实世界评估的基准，提供了衡量策略在真实物理操作中泛化能力的实用测试平台。

Conclusion: UMI-Bench 填补了 UMI 策略从数据到部署端到端评估的空白，为标准化真实机器人评估提供了可复现、可审计的测试框架。

Abstract: Real-robot evaluation is essential for understanding whether learned manipulation policies can operate reliably outside curated demonstrations. This need is particularly pressing for Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI)-style policies, whose performance depends on the coupling between wrist-view observations, action representation, data collection, and physical deployment. Existing real-world benchmarks have made important progress, but they are not designed around this UMI data-to-deployment setting. We present UMI-Bench 1.0, a local-first real-robot benchmark for standardized evaluation of UMI-style manipulation policies. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first benchmark dedicated to real-world evaluation of UMI-based manipulation models. UMI-Bench aligns data collection, scene reset, policy execution, result logging, and task-factor analysis within a unified protocol. By making the full evaluation process reproducible and auditable, UMI-Bench provides a practical testbed for measuring how UMI-trained policies generalize to real physical manipulation.

</details>


### [116] [Information-Preserving Continuous Occupancy Mapping with Variance-Weighted Submap Joining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10442)
*Zhuhua Bai,Yingyu Wang,Liang Zhao,Shoudong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个连续概率子图拼接框架，在隐log-odds空间联合优化子图位姿与全局占据场，使用信息保持的稀疏贝叶斯公式压缩观测数据为充分统计量，实现闭式预测均值和方差，相比离散网格方法在精度和全局一致性上更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有占据栅格子图拼接方法基于离散网格，导致优化梯度非光滑且忽略占据估计的不确定性，需要更连续、概率化的处理方式。

Method: 提出连续概率子图拼接框架，在log-odds隐空间联合优化子图位姿与全局占据场；采用信息保持的稀疏贝叶斯公式将原始观测压缩为充分统计量log-odds元组，保留后验信息；推导闭式预测均值与方差，实现带解析雅可比的子图拼接优化，位姿收敛后生成闭式最优全局地图。

Result: 在仿真和大规模真实数据集上，相比最先进的基于网格的子图拼接方法实现了更高的位姿精度和更好的全局一致性，同时比现有连续占据映射方法产生更紧凑的地图表示和更校准的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 连续概率子图拼接框架有效解决了离散网格方法的梯度平滑性和不确定性建模问题，实现了更精确的SLAM全局一致性。

Abstract: Large-scale SLAM remains challenging due to accumulated trajectory drift and the increasing computational cost of maintaining global consistency. Submap joining alleviates these issues by constructing locally consistent submaps and subsequently fusing them into a global map. However, existing occupancy-based submap joining methods operate on discrete grids, resulting in non-smooth gradients during optimization and neglecting the uncertainty associated with occupancy estimates. We propose the first continuous probabilistic submap joining framework that jointly optimizes submap poses and a global occupancy field in the latent log-odds space. The framework employs an information-preserving sparse Bayesian formulation that compresses raw occupancy observations into sufficient-statistic log-odds tuples while retaining the posterior information of the original observations. This yields closed-form predictive mean and variance estimates for occupancy mapping, which directly enable a submap joining formulation with analytical Jacobians, leading to more accurate submap joining and yielding a closed-form optimal global map upon pose convergence. Experiments on both simulated and large-scale real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed method achieves higher pose accuracy and improved global consistency than state-of-the-art grid-based submap joining approaches, while producing more compact map representations and better-calibrated uncertainty estimates than existing continuous occupancy mapping methods.

</details>


### [117] [GuideWalk: Learning Unified Autonomous Navigation and Locomotion for Humanoid Robots across Versatile Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10449)
*Haoxuan Han,Chen Chen,Linao Gong,Xin Yang,Hao Hu,Junhong Guo,Zhicheng He,Yao Su,Fenghua He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GuideWalk 是一个端到端的人形机器人导航框架，结合了可通行性感知的导航引导与地形自适应 locomotion 教师策略，通过复合教师蒸馏和强化学习微调实现稳定导航与动态可行运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人在复杂地形上导航时，障碍物避让与动态可行运动需要协同，但缺乏统一框架。

Method: 提出导航模块提供显式速度引导解耦避障与地形条件；复合教师蒸馏方案聚合目标导向指令与动态一致动作并蒸馏为单一策略；结合强化学习与辅助行为克隆目标进行策略优化。

Result: GuideWalk 实现了稳定有效的导航，同时保持了稳定的人形机器人 locomotion。

Conclusion: 端到端框架能够有效协调导航与地形自适应运动，提升人形机器人在多样环境中的导航能力。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have achieved strong locomotion capabilities, but reliable navigation on versatile terrains remains challenging because obstacle avoidance must be coordinated with dynamically feasible motion. In this work, we present GuideWalk, a unified end-to-end framework that integrates traversability-aware navigation guidance with terrain-adaptive locomotion teacher for humanoid navigation. Specifically, we introduce a navigation module that provides explicit velocity guidance, decoupling obstacle avoidance from terrain conditions to enable robust planning across diverse environments. We propose a composite teacher distillation scheme, where goal-directed commands and dynamically consistent actions are aggregated and distilled into a single policy. To further improve robustness, the distilled policy is refined with reinforcement learning and an auxiliary behavior cloning objective, which promotes exploration while preserving desirable teacher behaviors. Experiments demonstrate that GuideWalk achieves stable and effective navigation while maintaining stable humanoid locomotion.

</details>


### [118] [Act on What You See: Unlocking Safe Social Navigation in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10495)
*Qingzi Wang,Xiyang Wu,Guangyao Shi,Dianwei Chen,Xianfeng Yang,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SALSA，一种两阶段无标注后训练框架，通过社会行为对齐和时间安全对齐，让预训练VLA模型利用其已具备的内部表征实现更安全的社交导航，减少86.4%的近碰撞事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练VLA模型内部已编码人与障碍物区分及未来碰撞信号，但行为克隆无法将这些信号转化为社交上合适的行动，存在表征与动作生成之间的不匹配。

Method: 两阶段无标注后训练框架：(1) 社会行为对齐——连接中间层社交特征到动作头，在反事实人-物场景对训练以打破视觉显著性捷径；(2) 时间安全对齐——自动生成未来风险监督，实现预期性碰撞避免。

Result: 在SCAND和真实部署中，近碰撞减少86.4%，社会反事实准确率从53%提升至93%。

Conclusion: 预训练VLA策略可通过更好地对齐其潜在表征与动作生成来适应更安全的社交导航，无需额外标注。

Abstract: Safe social navigation requires robots to distinguish people from ordinary obstacles and to react before danger becomes imminent. We show that pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models already encode pedestrian-object distinctions and future collision signals in their internal representations, but behavior cloning fails to translate these signals into socially appropriate actions. To address this mismatch, we propose SALSA, a two-stage annotation-free post-training framework: (1) social behavioral alignment bridges intermediate-layer social features to the action head and trains on counterfactual human-object scene pairs to break visual saliency shortcuts; (2) temporal safety alignment provides automatically generated future-risk supervision to enable anticipatory collision avoidance. On SCAND and real-world deployment, SALSA reduces near-collisions by 86.4% and improves social counterfactual accuracy from 53% to 93%, demonstrating that safer social navigation can be achieved by teaching VLA policies to act on representations they already possess. These results show that pretrained VLA policies can be adapted for safer social navigation by better aligning their latent representations with action generation.

</details>


### [119] [Uncovering Vulnerability of Vision-Language-Action Models under Joint-Level Physical Faults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10501)
*Minsoo Jo,Taeju Kwon,Junha Chun,Youngjoon Jeong,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究VLA模型在机器人关节级物理故障下的脆弱性，并提出J-PARC——一种轻量级残差校准框架，通过推断潜在故障状态并调节残差校准器，使冻结的VLA策略能够自适应修正动作，提升对关节退化、摩擦等故障的鲁棒性，同时保持无故障环境性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人系统面临执行器退化、硬件故障、安全限制、碰撞损坏等关节级物理故障，这些故障会改变策略的动作-运动映射关系，破坏闭环执行。已有VLA研究主要关注感知和语义鲁棒性，忽略了执行端物理故障对策略性能的影响。

Method: 提出J-PARC（Joint-level Physical-fault Aware Residual Calibrator），在冻结的VLA策略基础上构建轻量级残差校准框架：首先从近期的关节动力学数据推断潜在的关节故障状态（latent joint-fault regime），然后基于该状态条件化一个共享的残差校准器，对不同故障关节的动作进行自适应校正。

Result: 实验表明，J-PARC在多种关节级物理故障下显著提升了VLA策略的鲁棒性，同时保持无故障环境下的性能不退化。研究还揭示了故障的关节依赖效应——不同关节的退化对任务成功率的影响具有异质性，且性能下降不能仅归因于物理不可行性，可行故障（如摩擦增大）也会导致成功率显著下降。

Conclusion: VLA模型在执行端面临关节级物理故障的脆弱性不可忽视，J-PARC作为一种轻量级后置校准方案，能够在不修改原始VLA策略的前提下有效提升其对执行故障的鲁棒性，为VLA在真实机器人系统中的可靠部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Deploying Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models in real robotic systems requires robustness not only to semantic and perceptual variations, but also to embodiment-side faults that change how actions are physically realized. Real robots can experience joint-level changes caused by actuator degradation, hardware faults, safety limits, collision damage, or wear-induced friction. These faults are critical because they alter the action-to-motion interface of a policy, disrupting the learned closed-loop relationship between commanded actions, realized motion, and subsequent observations. In this work, we study realistic joint-level physical faults and show that VLA models are vulnerable when predicted actions are executed through a perturbed robot body. Our analysis reveals joint-dependent effects, with heterogeneous degradation in task success across affected joints. We also show that performance drops cannot be attributed solely to physical infeasibility, since feasible faults such as increased joint friction can still substantially reduce success rates and induce closed-loop execution mismatch. Motivated by these findings, we propose Joint-level Physical-fault Aware Residual Calibrator (J-PARC), a lightweight residual calibration framework built on top of a frozen VLA policy. J-PARC infers a latent joint-fault regime from recent joint dynamics and conditions a shared residual calibrator on this regime, enabling adaptive action correction across faulty joints. Experiments show that J-PARC improves robustness under joint-level faults while preserving fault-free environment performance.

</details>


### [120] [VeriSpace: Spatially Grounded Action Verification for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10568)
*Guiyu Zhao,Longteng Guo,Junyou Zhu,Jun Fu,Yanghong Mei,Bin Cao,Jie Jiang,Xingjian He,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VeriSpace，一种3D感知的动作验证器，用于VLA系统的测试时动作选择。通过双路径3D注入场景编码和空间基础动作推理两个关键组件，在候选动作中区分细微几何差异并评估任务进展，显著提升VLA策略的决策可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操作中一次动作预测可靠性有限，微小动作误差可能导致抓取失败、碰撞或任务错误进展。需要测试时验证机制来评估多个候选动作。

Method: VeriSpace包含两个核心组件：(1) 双路径3D注入场景编码——联合保留视觉语义和显式3D几何的场景表示；(2) 空间基础动作推理——通过推理任务相关的空间关系、几何有效性和预期目标进度来评估每个动作。

Result: 在公开基准和真实世界机器人操作任务上，VeriSpace在分布内和分布外设置下均持续优于底层VLA策略和先前基于验证的方法，取得显著提升。

Conclusion: VeriSpace能够更可靠地区分细微但结果关键的候选动作，同时与现有VLA策略完全兼容，是一种有效的测试时动作选择方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown strong promise for robotic manipulation, but their reliability at test time remains limited by one-shot action prediction, where even small action errors can cause grasp failure, collision, or incorrect task progression. A natural alternative is to equip VLA systems with test-time verification, allowing multiple candidate actions to be proposed and evaluated before execution. However, reliable action verification is challenging because it requires not only distinguishing subtle geometric differences between candidate actions, but also assessing whether an action makes meaningful progress toward the task goal. We present VeriSpace, a 3D-aware action verifier for test-time action selection in VLA systems. VeriSpace evaluates candidate actions through two key components: Dual-Path 3D-Injected Scene Encoding, which constructs a scene representation that jointly preserves visual semantics and explicit 3D geometry, and Spatially-Grounded Action Reasoning, which evaluates each action by reasoning over task-relevant spatial relations, geometric validity, and expected goal progress. Together, these components enable more reliable discrimination between subtle yet outcome-critical action candidates while remaining fully compatible with existing VLA policies. Experiments on public benchmarks and real-world robotic manipulation tasks show that VeriSpace consistently improves decision reliability over both underlying VLA policies and prior verification-based methods, yielding substantial gains in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings.

</details>


### [121] [AgenticNav: Zero-Shot Vision-and-Language Navigation as a Tool-Calling Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10577)
*Yijian Li,Changze Li,Hantian Shi,Jiaying Luo,Jiyuan Cai,Ming Yang,Tong Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AgenticNav 将零样本 VLN-CE 重新设计为 VLM 与环境之间的智能体接口，通过将动作、深度和记忆暴露为可调用工具（直接像素选择动作、按需深度查询、紧凑地图记忆+选择性回放），在 R2R-CE 上达到零样本 SOTA，并在真实世界验证中展现泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本 VLN-CE 方法依赖学习到的航点预测器（限制动作空间且无法有效利用深度输入），记忆管理通过累积大量长文本/视觉历史或跨 episode 检索来弱化零样本设定。作者希望解决这些限制，让 VLM 更直接地与连续环境交互。

Method: 提出 AgenticNav 框架：1) 动作工具：VLM 直接选择 RGB 观察中的目标像素，转换为可执行运动，替代航点预测器；2) 深度工具：按需查询像素级精确度量距离；3) 记忆工具：紧凑地图图像总结历史轨迹 + 召回工具允许 VLM 选择性回溯过去视觉观察，避免提示上下文过载。

Result: 在 R2R-CE 基准上，使用相同 VLM 骨干网络达到零样本 SOTA；真实世界验证展示了优于先前方法的零样本泛化能力；消融实验证实动作工具设计优于传统航点预测器，深度工具和智能体记忆进一步提升了导航性能。

Conclusion: 通过将 VLN-CE 重新定义为 VLM 与环境的智能体接口，利用可调用工具（动作、深度、记忆）能够显著提升零样本连续环境导航性能，是一种轻量级且有效的导航框架。

Abstract: Zero-shot vision-and-language navigation in continuous environments (VLN-CE) has recently become feasible with large vision-language models (VLMs). However, existing methods typically rely on learned waypoint predictors to propose navigable actions. This severely limits the model's action space and fails to leverage depth inputs effectively. Moreover, memory is commonly handled by accumulating long textual or visual histories with substantial irrelevant context, or by retrieving cross-episode experiences, which weakens the zero-shot setting. In this paper, we rethink zero-shot VLN-CE as an agentic interface between the VLM and the environment, and present AgenticNav, a lightweight navigation harness that exposes action, depth, and memory as callable tools. Instead of choosing from predicted waypoints, the action tool allows the VLM to directly select a target pixel in RGB observations, converting it into executable motion. Depth is exposed through an on-demand pixel-depth tool, enabling the VLM to request precise metric distances only where they matter. For memory, AgenticNav provides a compact map image summarizing the historical trajectory, paired with a recall tool that allows the VLM to selectively revisit past visual observations without overwhelming the prompt context. On the R2R-CE benchmark, AgenticNav establishes new state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance among zero-shot methods given the same VLM backbone. Real-world validation further highlights its zero-shot generalization compared to prior methods. Ablations show that our action tool design outperforms traditional waypoint predictors, and that depth tool and agentic memory further contribute to navigation performance.

</details>


### [122] [LieIPM: Lie Group Interior Point Method for Direct Trajectory Optimization of Rigid Bodies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10579)
*Sangli Teng,Ruiqi Zhang,Tzu-Yuan Lin,William A Clark,Mark Mueller,Ram Vasudevan,Maani Ghaffari,Koushil Sreenath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于矩阵李群的约束轨迹优化框架LieIPM，利用李群结构实现二阶刚体模型上的牛顿型更新，并通过李群内点法处理流形上的约束，避免了旋转运动拓扑中的奇异性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束优化器通常在欧氏空间中运行，忽略了刚体运动的流形结构，导致奇异性或优化问题病态。本文旨在弥合这一差距，开发一种直接作用于矩阵李群的结构感知约束轨迹优化框架。

Method: 基于李群结构的二阶刚体模型，推导闭式内在导数以利用群对称性；结合李群变分积分器，提出线搜索李群内点法(LieIPM)在流形上处理约束。

Result: 数值实验表明LieIPM相比通用求解器和结构利用最优控制方法具有更优的鲁棒性和更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: LieIPM通过构造保留了旋转运动的拓扑结构，避免了奇异性，为刚体轨迹优化提供了更几何合理且高效的求解方案。

Abstract: Designing dynamically feasible trajectories for rigid bodies is a fundamental problem in robotics. While direct methods are widely used, the existing constrained optimizers typically operate in Euclidean space and ignore the manifold structure of rigid body motions. This mismatch may introduce singularities or lead to poorly conditioned optimization problems. To bridge this gap, we develop a structure-aware framework for constrained trajectory optimization directly on matrix Lie groups. Our approach is based on the second-order rigid body models utilizing Lie group structures, which enables efficient Newton-type updates while preserving the underlying geometry. Building on this model, we propose a line-search Lie Group Interior Point Method (LieIPM) to handle constraints on the manifolds. We instantiate the framework for rigid body motion planning using Lie group variational integrators and derive closed-form intrinsic derivatives that exploit group symmetries. The LieIPM preserves the topology of rotation motions by construction and avoids singularities. Numerical results demonstrate superior robustness and faster convergence compared to general-purpose solvers and structure-exploiting optimal control methods.

</details>


### [123] [Dexterous Point Policy: Learning Point-based Dexterous Hand Policies from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10614)
*Beomjun Kim,Seong Hyeon Park,Seunghoon Sim,Seungjun Moon,Sanghyeok Lee,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Dexterous Point Policy (DPP) 框架，直接从人类视频中学习灵巧操作策略，无需机器人演示。核心思路是用统一3D关键点表示（手腕和指尖）桥接人-机器人具身差异，训练自回归Transformer实现策略直接迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型预训练后存在具身差距，需要机器人专用演示微调，但灵巧操作（如多指手遥操作）的数据收集极其昂贵耗时。本文旨在消除对机器人演示的需求，直接从人类视频学习。

Method: 从原始视频中提取任务相关物体和人类手的3D关键点，训练自回归Transformer在关键点序列上建模。关键洞察是在关键点层级（特别是手腕和指尖），人类和机器人行为高度对齐，从而实现直接策略迁移。

Result: 在真实机器人抓取放置和工具使用任务上达到75.0%成功率，而最先进VLA基线仅1.0%；强泛化到未见场景，包括多物体环境和新型物体类别。

Conclusion: 3D关键点表示可以有效弥合人-机器人的具身差距，无需机器人演示即可实现灵巧操作策略学习，且泛化能力强。

Abstract: Robotic foundation models pre-trained on human demonstration videos have shown promise, but a significant embodiment gap remains when the resulting policies are deployed on real robots. A common remedy is to fine-tune these models on robot-specific demonstrations. However, robot data collection can be prohibitively expensive and time-consuming, which is particularly acute in dexterous manipulation, e.g., teleoperating a multi-fingered hand for even a single atomic task can take days. To address this, we introduce Dexterous Point Policy, a framework that learns dexterous manipulation policies directly from human videos and requires no robot demonstrations. Our core insight is that a unified 3D keypoint representation can bridge human and robot embodiments when used for both observations and actions. Specifically, we extract 3D keypoints of task-relevant objects and human hands from raw videos, and train an autoregressive transformer over these keypoints. We observe that at the keypoint level, specifically the wrist and fingertips, human and robot behaviors closely align, enabling direct policy transfer. On a suite of real-robot tasks spanning pick-and-place and tool use, Dexterous Point Policy attains 75.0% success, whereas a state-of-the-art VLA baseline reaches only 1.0%. Furthermore, our method generalizes strongly to unseen scenarios, including multi-object environments and novel object categories.

</details>


### [124] [Planar-Sector LOS Guidance for Interception of Agile Targets with Lifting-Wing Quadcopters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10639)
*Linkai Liu,Kun Yang,Han Zou,Chen Min,Shuli Lv,Shuai Wang,Quan Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种平面扇形视场（PS-LOS）制导框架，用于仅搭载单目相机的升力翼四旋翼对敏捷空中目标的自主视觉拦截。与传统圆锥视场约束不同，PS-LOS仅严格约束横向图像误差，放松纵向误差，在保持目标可见性的同时释放更多推力用于追击，显著提高了拦截性能。在户外实验中实现了138米距离的自主拦截。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有strapdown相机拦截方法使用圆锥视场约束保持目标靠近图像中心，这种对称约束不必要地限制了机动性，减少了可用于追击的推力。受FPV飞手不在所有图像方向保持相等可见性余量的观察启发，希望设计一种非对称约束来平衡可见性和机动性。

Method: 提出Planar-Sector LOS (PS-LOS)制导框架：严格约束横向图像误差，放松纵向图像误差到安全FOV余量内；结合延迟补偿的状态估计框架（无需直接深度测量）；以及面向升力翼四旋翼的非线性制导与控制架构。

Result: 相比传统圆锥LOS约束，PS-LOS在LOS方向附近提供近50%更多的可用推力；户外飞行实验成功拦截了具有大幅度、高频、不可预测运动的敏捷目标，最大拦截距离达138米，全程保持连续视觉跟踪。

Conclusion: PS-LOS是一种兼顾可见性保持与机动性的制导框架，适用于远程视觉拦截敏捷空中目标，实验验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous visual interception of agile aerial targets is challenging due to unpredictable target motion, limited sensing, and the strong coupling between target visibility and interceptor maneuverability. Most existing strapdown-camera interception methods preserve visibility using conic line-of-sight (LOS) constraints that keep the target near the image center. While safe, such symmetric constraints unnecessarily restrict maneuverability and can significantly reduce the usable thrust for pursuit. Motivated by the observation that aggressive FPV pilots do not maintain equal visibility margins in all image directions, this paper proposes a Planar-Sector Line-of-Sight (PS-LOS) guidance framework for autonomous interception using a lifting-wing quadcopter equipped with only a strapdown monocular camera. PS-LOS tightly constrains lateral image error while relaxing longitudinal image error within a safe field-of-view margin, preserving visibility while releasing maneuverability for acceleration-intensive pursuit. Under the lifting-wing quadcopter model, PS-LOS provides nearly 50% more available thrust near the LOS direction than conventional conic LOS constraints. To realize LOS-only interception without direct depth measurements, a delay-compensated state-estimation framework and a nonlinear guidance-and-control architecture are developed for lifting-wing quadcopters. Extensive outdoor flight experiments demonstrate autonomous interception of agile targets exhibiting large-amplitude, high-frequency, and unpredictable motion under real wind disturbances. The proposed system achieves successful interceptions at ranges up to 138 m while maintaining continuous visual tracking throughout the engagement. The results validate PS-LOS as a visibility-preserving, maneuverability-aware guidance framework for long-range visual interception of agile aerial targets.

</details>


### [125] [UniDexTok: A Unified Dexterous Hand Tokenizer from Real Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10683)
*Dong Fang,Youjun Wu,Yuanxin Zhong,Rui Zhang,Yunlong Wang,Xiaosong Jia,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一灵巧手模型UDHM和跨具身状态分词器UniDexTok，将异构灵巧手状态映射到共享22-DoF语义接口，实现无需重定向的亚毫米级重建，MPJAE和MPJPE误差分别降低98.98%和99.03%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手硬件设计差异大（运动学、关节定义、自由度不同），导致状态表示不统一，数据碎片化难以联合训练，缺乏像平行夹爪那样的共享表示。

Method: 提出UDHM作为22-DoF语义接口对齐人/机器人手状态；基于此设计UniDexTok，学习具身条件化的离散token，直接从真实关节状态标准化表示，无需重定向或仿真数据。

Result: 相比UniHM基线，MPJAE从15.63°降至0.16°（降98.98%），MPJPE从18.51mm降至0.18mm（降99.03%），达到亚毫米精度；跨具身数据能提升目标具身重建精度；具备零样本和少样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniDexTok有效统一异构灵巧手表示，实现了跨具身数据的联合训练收益和极低重建误差，为灵巧手数据标准化和规模化训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Dexterous hands are essential for fine-grained manipulation, but their hardware designs vary substantially across embodiments. Differences in kinematics, joint definitions, and degrees of freedom make it difficult to define a shared state representation compared with parallel grippers. As a result, dexterous-hand data remains fragmented and difficult to use for joint training. In this work, we propose the Unified Dexterous Hand Model (UDHM), which maps human and robot hand states into a shared 22-DoF semantic interface. Based on UDHM, we introduce UniDexTok, a retargeting-free state tokenizer that learns embodiment-conditioned discrete tokens from standardized real joint states. UniDexTok provides a unified representation for heterogeneous dexterous hands without relying on retargeting or simulation data. Compared with the recent baseline UniHM, UniDexTok reduces MPJAE from 15.63 degrees to 0.16 degrees and MPJPE from 18.51 mm to 0.18 mm, corresponding to error reductions of 98.98% and 99.03%, respectively. These results improve reconstruction from centimeter-scale to sub-millimeter accuracy. Experiments further show that data from other embodiments improves target-embodiment reconstruction accuracy, demonstrating the benefit of cross-embodiment tokenization. UniDexTok also shows strong zero-shot and few-shot reconstruction ability when new dexterous hands are introduced.

</details>


### [126] [Self-Supervised Relevance Modelling in Autonomous Driving via Counterfactual Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10688)
*Luca Lusvarghi,Javier Gozalvez,Pablo Urbano Hidalgo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于反事实分析的自监督方法，训练一个相关性模型，用于量化自动驾驶中物体的相关性，仅聚焦关键物体以提升计算效率、降低延迟并减少感知噪声传播。在合成因果数据集上验证，模型能以毫秒级延迟准确估计物体相关性，并生成相关性热图以辅助感知和规划决策。开源了模型和数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶感知管线计算密集，但并非所有检测到的物体都对驾驶决策有影响。识别和聚焦相关性高的物体可以更高效利用计算资源、降低处理延迟、减少下游感知噪声传播，但目前缺乏有效的物体相关性量化方法。

Method: 基于反事实分析（counterfactual analysis）的自监督方法。构建合成因果数据集，通过干预场景中物体"存在与否"来分析其对驾驶决策的因果影响，从而训练一个轻量级相关性模型，实现实时相关性估计。

Result: 在选定的城市场景合成数据上，相关性模型能以毫秒级延迟准确估计物体相关性，在高密度场景下也能实现实时估计。生成的相关性热图能提供对自动驾驶驾驶策略的洞察。

Conclusion: 反事实分析是一种有效的自监督方法用于学习物体相关性，能提升自动驾驶系统的计算效率和决策质量，且开源了模型和数据集。

Abstract: Autonomous driving relies on computationally intensive perception pipelines to continuously detect and track objects in the surrounding environment. While some objects are key to plan safe and effective maneuvers, others may not be relevant and have no impact on the autonomous vehicle's driving decisions. Focusing on relevant objects allows a more efficient usage of available computational resources, reduces processing latencies, and limits the downstream propagation of perception noise. In this work, we propose a novel self-supervised approach based on counterfactual analysis to develop a relevance model - an AI-based tool that quantifies the relevance of objects for an autonomous vehicle. To demonstrate the potential of the proposed approach, we train a relevance model on a synthetic causal dataset generated in a selected urban scenario. Results show that the relevance model is able to accurately estimate the objects' relevance with millisecond-level latency, enabling real-time relevance estimation also in high-density scenarios. We also show that the relevance model can be used to build relevance heatmaps that offer valuable insights into the autonomous vehicle's driving policy and can be used to proactively inform perception and planning tasks. We openly release both the relevance model and the causal dataset.

</details>


### [127] [Vehicle Prediction Model for Enhanced MPC Path Tracking in Formula Student Driverless](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10732)
*Sebastian Baader,Tamara Bergerhoff,Pascal Meißner,Frank Deinzer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于自动驾驶赛车的实时预测模型，将运动学自行车模型、离线贝叶斯线性回归和在线稀疏高斯过程回归三个子模型串联，在不显著增加计算成本的前提下提升预测精度，并在真实Formula Student赛车上验证了与MPC结合的路径跟踪控制效果，预测精度提升最高达57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶赛车接近物理极限行驶时呈现高度非线性行为，现有MPC方法依赖的预测模型精度不足，需要一种能自适应变化条件、实时且精度高的预测模型来提升路径跟踪性能。

Method: 将预测模型分解为三个子模型串联：1) 名义运动学自行车模型，2) 离线贝叶斯线性回归模型，3) 在线稀疏高斯过程回归模型，高效融合历史数据和当前驾驶信息，同时提供定量不确定性评估。

Result: 相比现有方法预测精度提升最高达57%，并在真实Formula Student赛车上成功验证了与MPC路径跟踪控制器结合的实用性。

Conclusion: 提出的三层串联预测模型能在不显著增加计算成本的情况下实现高精度预测和不确定性量化，适用于实际赛车场景中的MPC控制。

Abstract: Autonomous race cars, such as in Formula Student Driverless, operate close to their physical handling limits. The resulting highly nonlinear vehicle behavior increases the path tracking complexity, especially on narrow tracks. Model Predictive Control (MPC) is commonly used to address this issue, a method whose performance is closely tied to the accuracy of the underlying prediction model. This paper presents a novel, real-time capable prediction model for autonomous race cars that adjusts to changing conditions by combining information from past runs and the current driving situation. Our model is divided into three consecutive submodels: a nominal Kinematic Bicycle Model, an offline Bayesian Linear Regression (BLR) model, and an online Sparse Gaussian Process Regression (SGPR) model. The proposed approach enables efficient integration of all available data without significantly increasing computational cost, ensuring high prediction accuracy and a quantitative uncertainty assessment right from the start of the run. Compared to existing approaches, an improvement in prediction accuracy of up to 57% was achieved. Further, we successfully demonstrated the practical applicability of the model within an MPC-based path tracking controller on a real Formula Student race car.

</details>


### [128] [Pushing the Performance Limits in Autonomous Racing: Continuous Stability-Aware Adaptive Velocity Planning in Formula Student Driverless](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10733)
*Tamara Bergerhoff,Sebastian Baader,Pascal Meißner,Frank Deinzer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应速度规划方法，通过从车辆稳定性间接推断连续缩放因子而非直接估计轮胎-道路摩擦系数，生成连续摩擦图用于实时最优目标速度计算，在真实大学生方程式赛车上实现35%圈时提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主赛车中速度规划受赛道布局、环境影响、机械公差及控制不精确等多因素制约，传统方法难以实时动态适应变化条件。

Method: 不直接估计轮胎-道路摩擦系数，而是从车辆稳定性间接推断连续缩放因子，该因子反映有效轮胎-道路交互及控制不精确的影响，据此生成连续摩擦图作为自适应基础计算最优目标速度。

Result: 在真实大学生方程式赛车上评估，相比非自适应方法10圈内圈时提升35%，平均提升8%。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应速度规划方法可以有效处理动态变化条件，大幅提升圈速性能。

Abstract: In autonomous racing, especially in competitions such as Formula Student Driverless, precise planning of the target velocity of a race car is crucial for competitive lap times and stable driving behavior. Especially at high speeds, Velocity Planning (VP) is a significant challenge as it has to be performed in real time, taking into account track layouts, environmental influences, mechanical tolerances, and the resulting control inaccuracies. In this paper, we present a novel approach to VP that dynamically adapts to such changing conditions. Instead of estimating the physical Tire-Road Friction Coefficient (TRFC), a continuous scaling factor is inferred indirectly from vehicle stability. This factor not only reflects the effective tire-road interaction but also captures effects of control inaccuracies. From this, we generate a continuous friction map, which serves as a robust, adaptive basis for computing the optimal target speed, accounting for both vehicle and environmental limits. Our proposed approach was evaluated on a real Formula Student race car, showing a lap time improvement of 35 % over ten laps and an average increase of 8 % compared to a non-adaptive approach.

</details>


### [129] [Hand-centric Human-to-Robot Trajectory Transfer from Video Demonstrations via Open-World Contact Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10743)
*Yitian Shi,Di Wen,Zhengqi Han,Zicheng Guo,Yu Hu,Edgar Welte,Kunyu Peng,Rainer Stiefelhagen,Rania Rayyes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HOWTransfer是一种从人类视频演示中提取机器人可执行轨迹的手部中心框架，通过接触感知的3D手部运动恢复、时间接触区间定位、抓取意图重定向到平行夹爪以及轨迹编辑，实现跨物体、跨 embodimnent 的开放世界技能迁移，成功率达86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类示教视频中存在手物交互噪声、物体部分遮挡和跨具身差异，现有方法依赖物体特定描述或显式状态跟踪，泛化性差。作者希望利用手部本身作为鲁棒的交互线索，实现免物体模型、免语言查询的开放世界机器人技能迁移。

Method: 1) 从视频中恢复时间一致3D手部运动并定位接触时间区间；2) 利用接触起始点将人类抓取意图重定向为多模态平行夹爪抓取假设；3) 沿恢复的手腕轨迹传播抓取假设，生成机器人可执行运动；4) 轨迹编辑阶段优化接触对齐，从单次演示生成多种可执行变体。

Result: 在多种操作任务中，接触定位准确，机器人运动重定向成功率达86%，在盲选偏好研究中优于遥操作轨迹。

Conclusion: HOWTransfer验证了仅利用手部交互线索即可实现开放世界机器人技能迁移，无需物体先验或语言查询，在成功率与用户偏好上均优于基线方法。

Abstract: Learning from human video demonstrations remains challenging due to noisy hand-object interactions, unseen objects with partial observation, and cross-embodiment discrepancy. To address these challenges, we present \textit{HOWTransfer} (\emph{H}and-\emph{O}bject \emph{O}pen-\emph{W}orld Transfer), a hand-centric framework that distills human demonstrations into contact-aware, taxonomy-informed, and diverse robotic trajectories. Instead of relying on object-specific descriptions, vision-language queries, or explicit object-state tracking, \emph{HOWTransfer} recovers temporally consistent 3D hand motion and localizes temporal contact intervals by reasoning over observed hand-object interaction cues. The localized contact onsets are then used to retarget human grasp intent into multi-modal parallel-jaw grasp hypotheses, which are propagated along the recovered wrist trajectory to generate robot-executable motions. Finally, a trajectory editing stage refines contact alignment and produces diverse executable variants from a single demonstration. Experiments across diverse manipulation tasks show that \emph{HOWTransfer} enables accurate contact localization and high-quality robot motion retargeting with $86\%$ success, which is preferred over teleoperated trajectories in a blinded preference study.

</details>


### [130] [ros2probe: Non-intrusive, Kernel-selective Observability for Robot Operating System 2 Middleware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10746)
*Jisang Yu,Sanghoon Lee,Yeonwoo Choi,Kyung-Joon Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROS 2 的观测工具因 DDS 协议必须加入域作为订阅者，会引入探针效应（膨胀发现平面、增加反序列化开销、报告失准、近饱和时挤占真实订阅者消息）。ros2probe 通过被动捕获发现包重构通信状态，再用内核级过滤仅提取用户关注的话题报文，消除了探针效应，在三个平台、两种 DDS 实现和七个机器人负载下，发现图误差在 0.5% 以内，饱和时不丢包，CPU 和内存分别降低 7 倍和 28 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ROS 2 观测工具必须作为 DDS 域节点加入系统，导致观测本身扰动被测系统（探针效应），膨胀发现平面、增加开销、使丢包报告失准，在嵌入式机器人上甚至使系统过载。

Method: ros2probe 采用非侵入式方法：首先通过被动监听域内发现报文解析完整的 ROS 2 通信状态（话题、节点、发布/订阅关系），然后基于此信息驱动内核级 BPF 过滤器，仅提取用户指定话题的报文，绕过 DDS 订阅链路，实现零带宽代价的观测。

Result: 在笔记本、Jetson、Raspberry Pi 三个平台，Fast DDS 和 Cyclone DDS 两种实现，七个机器人负载下：发现图误差保持在 0.5% 以内（传统工具膨胀至 2.6 倍）；饱和时零丢包（传统工具丢 38.5%）；丢包报告召回率 1.0；CPU 和内存分别降低 7 倍和 28 倍。

Conclusion: ros2probe 有效消除了 ROS 2 观测中的探针效应，在保持与标准工具兼容的同时大幅降低开销，尤其适合资源受限的嵌入式机器人平台，是一种实用的非侵入式观测方案。

Abstract: Robot Operating System 2 (ROS 2), the de facto standard middleware framework for robots, runs each robot as a graph of nodes communicating over the Data Distribution Service (DDS), a publish/subscribe substrate. Observing this inter-node communication in real time is essential to robot development, yet it has a price. A tool can receive data only by joining the DDS domain as a subscriber that discovery has matched to the publisher, so observing folds the tool into the system it measures and perturbs it. We define this protocol-inherent perturbation as the observer's probe effect. It inflates the discovery plane, adds deserialization cost on the observer, makes the loss it reports diverge from what the subscriber actually received, and near saturation displaces the subscriber's messages. The only escape, capturing all wire traffic passively, discards ROS 2 message semantics and scales with total traffic, not what is observed. We present ros2probe, a non-intrusive observation framework that removes the probe effect. It reconstructs the full ROS 2 communication state from the domain's discovery packets at no bandwidth cost, then drives an in-kernel filter restricted to the topics the user asks for, lifting only those packets at minimal cost and observing what the real subscriber receives. Its interfaces and recordings match the standard ROS 2 tools. Across three hardware platforms (laptop, Jetson, and Raspberry Pi), two DDS implementations, and seven robot-operation workloads, ros2probe holds the discovery graph within 0.5% of an unobserved system, whereas domain-joining tools inflate discovery up to 2.6$\times$ and drop 38.5% of the subscriber's messages at saturation while ros2probe drops none. It reports loss with a recall of 1.0, cuts observer CPU and memory by up to 7$\times$ and 28$\times$, and stays practical on the embedded robots where existing tools overload the system.

</details>


### [131] [Bridging Semantics and Physical Execution: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Multi-Pair Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10808)
*Xinyi Li,Aiguo Song,Linhu Wei,Huijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出端到端神经符号框架，通过分层生成最优子图、解耦通用性与边缘情况、解决跨对干扰，在100个真实场景离线评估中达97%全局可执行性，UR3机械臂实际部署达90%成功率（0.5mm公差）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在认知决策与物理执行之间存在鸿沟，要么遭遇状态空间爆炸和知识瓶颈，要么出现逻辑幻觉和拓扑冲突，无法解决非结构化环境中的多对机器人装配问题。

Method: 端到端神经符号框架：(1) 使用RGB-D提取语义实例状态并量化场景散度；(2) 为每对生成最优子图（LLM + 基本动作抑制幻觉）；(3) 轻量判别器推理边缘情况支撑动作；(4) 拓扑协调全局序列；(5) 动态行为树嵌入原子技能闭环力感知执行。

Result: 100个真实场景离线评估97%全局可执行性，超越经典和SOTA规划器；UR3实际部署90%成功率，0.5mm公差下强干扰仍有效。

Conclusion: 提出的方法为复杂自主装配提供了统一且可验证的解决方案，有效桥接了认知决策与物理执行之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Multi-pair robotic assembly in unstructured environments faces spatial interference and contact uncertainties. Existing paradigms fail to bridge cognitive decision-making and physical execution, as they either encounter state-space explosion and knowledge bottlenecks or suffer from logical hallucinations and topological conflicts. We propose an end-to-end neuro-symbolic framework that solves the challenge hierarchically: generating optimal subgraphs for each pair, decoupling generality from edge cases, and then resolving cross-pair interferences. Given an eye-on-hand RGB-D assembly scene, the framework extracts semantic instance identity and state while quantifying the scene for divergence calculation. For each pair, optimal subgraph is generated via LLM using barely basic actions to mitigate hallucinations. Supportive actions for edge cases are reasoned and inserted with a lightweight discriminator. Driven by the divergence between the quantified baseline and current scene, it is easily extensible at low cost. Augmented subgraphs are topologically coordinated into global sequences while preserving internal behavioral coherence. Dynamic behavior trees embedding atomic skills close the force-aware execution loop. Offline evaluation on 100 real-world scenes achieves 97.00% global executability, outperforming classical and state-of-the-art planners. Real-robot deployment on a UR3 arm attains 90% success rate with 0.5 mm tolerance under strong interference, demonstrating a unified and verifiable solution for complex autonomous assembly.

</details>


### [132] [IMPACT: Learning Internal-Model Predictive Control for Forceful Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10818)
*Jiawei Gao,Chaoqi Liu,Peilin Wu,Haonan Chen,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出IMPACT框架，将带力交互的机器人操作任务解耦为任务规划与基于内模型的预测控制，在仿真和真实实验中实现了更高成功率、对未见物体重量的更好泛化性，以及更好的安全性和能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，隐式方法（通过稳态误差实现力交互）在不同物体重量下泛化性差，显式方法（使用腕力/力矩或触觉传感器）需要专用硬件且增加系统复杂度，缺乏一种无需额外硬件且能良好泛化的力交互操作方案。

Method: 提出IMPACT框架，将带力交互的任务解耦为两个部分：任务规划（task-planning）和基于内模型的预测控制（internal-model-based predictive control），通过内模型预测实现力交互而不依赖外部力传感器。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，IMPACT在成功率、对未见物体重量的泛化性、安全性和能效方面均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: IMPACT通过解耦任务规划与内模型预测控制，在无需额外力/触觉传感器的前提下，实现了对重量变化具有鲁棒性的力交互操作，兼具安全性和能效优势。

Abstract: Real-world robotic manipulation tasks often involve forceful interactions with the environment, such as using tools of varying weights, transporting objects with different masses, and performing contact-rich tasks like table wiping. Previous learning-based approaches typically employ imitation learning policies that output target end-effector poses tracked by low-level impedance controllers. In these systems, forceful interactions are either implicitly realized through steady-state tracking errors or explicitly commanded using wrist force/torque or tactile sensors. However, implicit approaches generalize poorly across object weights, while explicit approaches require specialized hardware and increase system complexity. In this work, we propose IMPACT, a framework that decouples these forceful tasks into task-planning and internal-model-based predictive control. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves higher success rates and improved generalization to unseen object weights, as well as better safety and energy efficiency.

</details>


### [133] [GUIDE: Goal-Initialized Directional Understanding for End-to-End Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10832)
*Liang Wang,Jin Jin,KanZhong Yao,YiBin Wu,Fangqiang Ding,Jin Wang,Jun Wu,Zhe Sun,Qiuguo Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GUIDE，一个完全端到端的强化学习框架，使四足机器人仅凭初始目标指令和内在空间记忆进行导航，无需后续目标更新或先验地图。通过多频率本体感受历史提取自我运动表示，结合原始深度流感知局部几何，在仿真和真实场景中实现可靠导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的四足机器人视觉导航过度依赖层级状态估计的连续目标更新，增加了感知和计算开销，偏离了完全端到端自主性。且在部分可观测条件下，策略容易学习短视行为，陷入死角和复杂结构布局。

Method: 提出GUIDE框架：(1) 空间锚点预测器，利用多频率本体感受历史提取自我运动表示，维持长程空间上下文；(2) 利用原始深度流感知局部环境几何。两者结合在强化学习框架中端到端训练。

Result: 在四足机器人的仿真和真实场景实验中，GUIDE学习了可靠的自我运动和方向感知能力，使完全端到端部署的策略能够在密集杂乱环境和结构化迷宫中安全导航，无需后续目标引导或先验地图。

Conclusion: GUIDE证明了仅凭初始目标即可实现四足机器人自主导航的可行性，通过内在空间记忆替代外部目标更新，推动了完全端到端移动自主性的发展。

Abstract: Learning-based visual navigation for legged robots typically relies on continuous goal updates from hierarchical state estimation to provide a persistent directional reference. This reliance incurs additional sensory and computational overhead and deviates from fully end-to-end mobile autonomy. Furthermore, under partial observability, policies are prone to learn myopic behaviors, easily becoming trapped in dead ends and complex structural layouts. To address these limitations, we investigate a goal-initialized navigation setting, where the target is provided only once at the beginning of an episode, requiring the robot to operate based on intrinsic spatial memory without subsequent goal updates from external modules. In this work, we propose GUIDE, a fully end-to-end reinforcement learning framework designed to cultivate internal directional awareness. Specifically, GUIDE incorporates a spatial anchor predictor that leverages multi-frequency proprioceptive history to extract egomotion representations, thereby maintaining a persistent long-horizon spatial context for navigation. Concurrently, it utilizes raw depth streams to perceive local environmental geometry. We evaluate the proposed framework across both simulation and real-world scenarios on a quadruped robot. Experiments show that GUIDE learns reliable egomotion and directional awareness, enabling a fully end-to-end deployed policy to safely navigate through dense clutter and structured mazes without subsequent goal guidance or prior maps.

</details>


### [134] [Gradient based Bilevel for Inverse Optimal Control, a Riemannian approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10841)
*Ahmed-Manaf Dahmani,Vincent Bonnet,David Daney,François Charpillet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出黎曼几何逆最优控制（RIOC）方法，将IOC问题重新表述为流形上的优化，避免了经典双层优化的计算负担和投影法的数值不稳定性，在真实人体手臂轨迹数据上计算速度提升约4倍，精度相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典IOC的双层优化计算代价高，而近期投影法在梯度求解时违反标准约束条件导致数值不稳定，需要更高效且数值稳定的方法。

Method: 理论证明满足最优性条件的轨迹集自然构成一个流形，将IOC重写为该流形上的优化问题，提出黎曼IOC方法，在保持约束可行性的同时将观测轨迹投影到最优解流形上。

Result: 在真实人体手臂轨迹实验中，RIOC与经典双层IOC重建精度相当或更优，计算时间减少约4倍。

Conclusion: 几何优化方法能有效提升IOC的可扩展性和数值可靠性，对机器人学和人体运动分析有重要潜力。

Abstract: Inverse Optimal Control (IOC) aims to recover the cost function that explains observed trajectories as solutions of an optimal control problem. Classical IOC formulations rely on bilevel optimization, which repeatedly solves a nested optimal control problem and quickly becomes computationally prohibitive for realistic systems. Recent projection-based approaches offer a promising alternative but suffer from numerical instability when solved with gradient-based methods due to violations of standard constraint qualifications.
  In this paper, we show that these difficulties stem from the geometric structure of the IOC feasible set. We demonstrate that the set of trajectories satisfying the optimality conditions naturally forms a manifold and reformulate IOC as an optimization problem on this manifold. Based on this insight, we propose a Riemannian Inverse Optimal Control (RIOC) method that projects observed trajectories onto the manifold of optimal solutions while preserving feasibility by construction. Experiments on real human arm trajectories show that the proposed method achieves comparable or better reconstruction accuracy than classical bilevel IOC while reducing computation time by about a factor of four. These results highlight the potential of geometric optimization methods to improve the scalability and reliability of IOC for robotics and human motion analysis.

</details>


### [135] [An Exposure-Time-Aligned Primary-Path Architecture for Autonomous-Driving ECUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10856)
*Toru Saito,Yuki Hagura,Tatsuya Konishi,Satoru Mizusawa,Takumi Yajima*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在量产自动驾驶的过渡阶段，提出了一种支持模块化流水线与端到端路径共存并渐进迁移的架构设计。核心三大原则：主路优先（Primary-Path）、曝光时间对齐（Exposure-Time-Aligned）及共路共存（Co-Path Coexistence），在双SoC量产AD-ECU上实现了296ms的平均延迟，满足350ms设计预算，并允许E2E路径以影子模式并行运行、逐步扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量产车仍依赖模块化多NN流水线，而学术界已转向端到端自动驾驶。如何在过渡期内设计一种架构，既能保留现有多模块流水线，又能引入E2E路径，并支持从模块化向E2E的分阶段迁移，是亟需解决的实际工程问题。

Method: 提出三条设计原则：(i) Primary-Path——显式选择一条主感知链，优先将其封闭在同一SoC对中；(ii) Exposure-Time-Aligned——将主传感器的曝光时间τ_exp作为标签沿链传播，融合节点按匹配的τ_exp事件驱动而非固定周期；(iii) Co-Path Coexistence——建立在(i)(ii)基础上，让E2E输出路径与模块化流水线在同一τ_exp周期内并行运行。

Result: 在双SoC量产AD-ECU上，实现了从相机快门到规划器输出的平均延迟296ms，满足350ms设计预算。在Co-Path Coexistence下，模块化流水线作为主路径，E2E路径以影子模式在实车上并行运行，随评估证据积累逐步扩大E2E范围。

Conclusion: 该架构在量产平台上证明了模块化与E2E路径共存与渐进迁移的可行性，为行业过渡期提供了一种务实且可落地的工程方案。

Abstract: While end-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving has become the dominant research direction, production vehicles continue to rely on modular multi-NN pipelines for a non-trivial transitional period. The subject of this paper is the design of an architecture that, during this phase, supports a modular pipeline and an E2E path side by side and embeds a path for staged migration. Transplanted to a production SoC, egalitarian late fusion is compute-inefficient and offers no natural unit for staged E2E substitution. As an alternative, we propose three design principles: (i) Primary-Path, which explicitly selects a primary perception chain and prioritizes its enclosure within a single SoC pair over the non-critical paths (ii) Exposure-Time-Aligned, which propagates the primary sensor's exposure time $τ_{\rm exp}$ as a tag along the chain and event-drives the fusion node on matched $τ_{\rm exp}$ rather than a fixed cycle and (iii) Co-Path Coexistence, which, building on (i) and (ii), lets an E2E output path co-run with the modular pipeline within the same $τ_{\rm exp}$ cycle. On a Dual-SoC production AD-ECU, the implementation closes camera-shutter to planner-output latency at a mean of 296 ms within the 350 ms design budget. Under (iii), the modular pipeline is primary at production launch and the E2E path runs as shadow on real vehicles, and the E2E scope is expanded as evaluation evidence accumulates.

</details>


### [136] [Embodiment-conditioned Generalist Control for Multirotor Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10857)
*Orestis Konstantaropoulos,Welf Rehberg,Mihir Kulkarni,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种通用的多旋翼位置控制策略，使用单一网络权重控制任意多旋翼构型（如六旋翼、四旋翼）。通过物理基础的形态描述符（质量与惯性归一化的控制分配矩阵）和PPO训练，在5分钟内完成训练，并在三种不同六旋翼系统上实现零样本真实世界迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多旋翼控制策略通常针对特定构型设计，缺乏跨形态泛化能力。需要一种通用控制策略，能在不同多旋翼构型（平面/非平面、对称/非对称）间迁移，减少重复训练成本。

Method: 使用质量与惯性归一化的控制分配矩阵作为形态描述符，将策略条件化于该描述符；在广泛分布的随机多旋翼构型上采样训练数据；采用PPO算法优化单一紧凑网络；基于NVIDIA Warp构建自定义动力学仿真器加速训练。

Result: 训练仅需5分钟（RTX 3090）；仿真实验中通用策略在任意形态下表现鲁棒；在三种不同的真实六旋翼系统（平面、部分对称非平面、随机非对称非平面）上实现零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 基于物理形态描述符的条件化策略能够有效实现跨多旋翼构型的通用控制，且无需微调即可迁移到真实世界，展示了强大的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: We present a generalist position control policy capable of controlling arbitrary multirotor configurations of a certain rotor count (e.g., hexarotors or quadrotors) with a single set of network weights. The policy is conditioned on a physics-grounded embodiment descriptor: a mass and inertia-normalized control allocation matrix that captures how mass-normalized motor thrusts generate linear and angular accelerations in the body-frame. To train the policy, we sample from a broad distribution of arbitrary multirotor configurations, including non-planar and asymmetric systems, and optimize a single, compact network using Proximal Policy Optimization. Training requires only five minutes on an RTX 3090 GPU using a custom NVIDIA Warp-based dynamics simulator. Through extensive simulation experiments, we show that embodiment conditioning enables robust generalist control across arbitrary morphologies. We demonstrate zero-shot real-world transfer of this generalist policy on three diverse hexarotor systems, including a planar robot, a partially symmetric non-planar system, and a random asymmetric, non-planar configuration.

</details>


### [137] [MV-Actor: Aligning Multi-View Semantics and Spatial Awareness for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10899)
*Yinchen Tian,Huan Li,Muyao Peng,Xi Wang,Yan Wang,You Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MV-Actor，一个面向双臂操作的多视角感知框架，通过多视角语义交互和语义-空间token交互构建统一的语义-空间表示，并引入引导度量深度修复模块处理消费级深度噪声，在PerAct2基准上达到87.8%的SOTA成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双臂操作的多视角策略要么独立编码每个视角，要么只是浅层融合视角特征，导致语义感知共享有限、空间感知不可靠，亟需更好的多视角统一表示方法。

Method: 1) 多视角语义交互模块共享跨视角语义感知；2) 语义-空间token交互将视觉语义与前馈重建模型特征对齐以获得可靠空间感知；3) 引导度量深度修复模块在消费级深度噪声下修复退化深度。

Result: PerAct2基准平均成功率87.8% SOTA；真实世界评估中在视角变化更频繁、深度不稳定条件下优于RGB和RGB-D基线。

Conclusion: 共享语义感知和可靠空间感知对双臂操作任务有显著收益，MV-Actor框架验证了这一方向的有效性。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation has been widely applied in industrial scenarios. Compared with single-arm manipulation, bimanual manipulation is equipped with multiple cameras to capture information from different viewpoints. However, existing multi-view policies encode each view independently or fuse view features shallowly, resulting in limited sharing semantic perception and unreliable spatial awareness. In this paper, we propose \textbf{MV-Actor}, a multi-view perception framework that builds a unified semantic-spatial representation for bimanual manipulation. First, MV-Actor performs Multi-view Semantic Interaction to share semantic perception across views. Then it uses Semantic-Spatial Token Interaction to ground visual semantics with feed-forward reconstruction model features and acquire reliable spatial awareness. Finally, a Guided Metric Depth Repair module refines degraded sensor depth to provide more reliable metric anchors under consumer-grade depth noise. In simulation experiments conducted on the PerAct2 bimanual benchmark, MV-Actor achieves a state-of-the-art average success rate of 87.8\%. In real-world evaluations with more frequent viewpoint changes and unstable consumer-grade depth, MV-Actor outperforms both RGB and RGB-D baselines, further demonstrating the benefit of sharing semantic perception and reliable spatial awareness for bimanual manipulation.

</details>


### [138] [AgniNav: Configuration-Driven Cross-Embodiment Local Planning for Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10903)
*Tianhao Zang,Siwei Cheng,Haidong Huang,Shanze Wang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AgniNav提出了一种基于碰撞包络配置的单目局部导航框架，通过四参数安全包络（高度、前长、后长、半宽）统一不同形态机器人（轮式、四足、人形）的感知与规划，无需重新训练即可实现跨形态迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉导航策略通常将感知耦合到特定机器人本体（相机高度、足迹等），从轮式迁移到足式平台需要重新训练或依赖主动深度传感器，缺乏统一跨形态迁移的标准化方案。

Method: 1) 定义四参数安全包络（碰撞高度、前长、后长、半宽）；2) 高度参数条件化图像到伪激光扫描网络（image-to-scan），从单目RGB预测一维碰撞相关扫描；3) 足迹参数配置维度感知局部规划器进行碰撞检测；4) 使用高度条件化的列最小值扫描标签（从配对的彩色-深度数据生成）训练，无需收集机器人特定数据。

Result: Turtlebot2上39/40成功/0碰撞，Unitree Go2上18/20成功/1碰撞，K1人形机器人上18/20成功/2碰撞，在Jetson Orin上以30Hz运行。

Conclusion: 首个在感知和规划上联合依赖于共享碰撞包络配置的单目局部导航框架，实现轮式、四足、人形平台零重训练部署。

Abstract: Monocular local navigation is attractive for lightweight robots, but existing vision-based policies often couple perception to a specific body, camera height, and footprint, making transfer from wheeled bases to legged platforms dependent on retraining or active depth hardware. This paper introduces AgniNav, a configuration-driven local navigation framework that standardizes cross-embodiment transfer at the collision-envelope level. Each robot is specified by a measurable four-parameter safety envelope: collision-relevant height, front length, rear length, and half width. The height parameter conditions an image-to-scan network to predict a one-dimensional, collision-relevant pseudo-laserscan from a monocular color image, while the remaining footprint parameters configure a dimension-aware local planner for collision checking. Training uses height-conditioned column-minimum scan labels generated from paired color-depth data, allowing the same image to supervise different safety envelopes without collecting robot-specific data. To the best of our knowledge, AgniNav is the first monocular local-navigation framework that jointly conditions perception and planning on a shared collision-envelope configuration for zero-retraining deployment across wheeled, quadruped, and humanoid platforms. Real-robot experiments on a Turtlebot2, Unitree Go2, and Accelerated Evolution K1 achieve 39/40, 18/20, and 18/20 successes with 0/40, 1/20, and 2/20 collisions, respectively, while running at 30 Hz on Jetson Orin.

</details>


### [139] [Task Robustness via Re-Labelling Vision-Action Robot Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10918)
*Artur Kuramshin,Özgür Aslan,Cyrus Neary,Glen Berseth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TREAD是一种利用大型视觉语言模型(VLM)对现有机器人数据集进行增强的框架，通过生成语义子任务、分割演示视频和生成多样化指令三个阶段，将长轨迹分解为语言-动作对，从而提升策略在未见过任务上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人学习策略在遵循指令方面表现不佳，主要是因为现有数据集中语言和动作序列的多样性不足，且收集新数据成本高昂。

Method: TREAD利用预训练VLM通过三个阶段增强数据：(1) 从原始指令和初始场景生成语义子任务；(2) 基于子任务条件分割演示视频；(3) 生成包含物体属性的多样化指令，将长演示分解为接地语言-动作对，并进一步通过语言多样的文本目标增强数据鲁棒性。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上的评估表明，使用TREAD增强数据集训练的策略在未见过的任务和目标上表现出改进的性能，在规划泛化和语言条件策略泛化方面均有提升。

Conclusion: TREAD通过轨迹分解增强规划泛化，通过增加语言多样性增强语言条件策略泛化，是一种可扩展的无需额外数据收集的数据增强方法。

Abstract: The recent trend in scaling models for robot learning has resulted in impressive policies that can perform various manipulation tasks and generalize to novel scenarios. However, these policies continue to struggle with following instructions, likely due to the limited linguistic and action sequence diversity in existing robotics datasets. This paper introduces Task Robustness via Re-Labelling Vision-Action Robot Data (TREAD), a scalable framework that leverages large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to augment existing robotics datasets without additional data collection, harnessing the transferable knowledge embedded in these models. Our approach leverages a pretrained VLM through three stages: generating semantic sub-tasks from original instruction labels and initial scenes, segmenting demonstration videos conditioned on these sub-tasks, and producing diverse instructions that incorporate object properties, effectively decomposing longer demonstrations into grounded language-action pairs. We further enhance robustness by augmenting the data with linguistically diverse versions of the text goals. Evaluations on LIBERO demonstrate that policies trained on our augmented datasets exhibit improved performance on novel, unseen tasks and goals. Our results show that TREAD enhances both planning generalization through trajectory decomposition and language-conditioned policy generalization through increased linguistic diversity.

</details>


### [140] [AllDayNav: Lifelong Navigation via Real-World Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10927)
*Hang Yin,Yinan Liang,Jiazhao Zhang,Jiahang Liu,Minghan Li,Zhizheng Zhang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AllDayNav提出了一种隐式编码场景动态的大模型终身导航框架，通过自进化多模态记忆和强化学习实现跨房间、跨情节、跨任务的鲁棒导航，无需显式地图或场景图，在合成和真实环境中接近100%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有依赖显式地图或场景图的终身导航方法在动态非结构化环境中泛化能力差，难以从碎片化观测中形成持久场景理解。

Method: 将场景动态隐式编码进大模型十亿级参数中，通过强化学习驱动；核心是自进化多模态记忆（维护视觉关键帧、语义描述、时间上下文），并自主生成开放词汇指令、图像目标和结构化奖励。

Result: 在跨房间、跨情节、跨任务场景中成功率接近100%，在路径效率和鲁棒性上持续超越基于地图、VLM和RL的强基线方法。

Conclusion: 隐式的、记忆驱动的强化学习可作为显式建图的可扩展替代方案，实现可靠的终身导航。

Abstract: Lifelong embodied navigation in dynamic environments requires robots to form persistent scene understanding from fragmentary observations, which remains difficult for existing methods that rely on explicit maps or scene graphs and struggle to generalize beyond structured settings. We propose AllDayNav, a lifelong self-learning navigation framework that implicitly encodes scene dynamics into the billion-scale parameters of a large model via reinforcement learning, powered by a self-evolving multimodal memory that maintains and updates visual keyframes, semantic descriptions, and temporal context while autonomously generating open-vocabulary instructions, image goals, and structured rewards. Experiments in both synthetic and real-world environments across cross-room, cross-episode, and cross-task scenarios show that AllDayNav achieves success rates approaching $100\%$ and consistently surpasses strong map-based, VLM, and RL baselines in path efficiency and robustness, demonstrating implicit, memory-driven reinforcement learning as a scalable alternative to explicit mapping for reliable lifelong navigation.

</details>


### [141] [Resilient Navigation for Autonomous Farm Robots by Leveraging Jerk-Augmented Models with IMU-Only Disturbance Rejection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10971)
*Batu Candan,Mohammed Atallah,Simone Servadio,Saeed Arabi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于加加速度增强EKF与多调谐因子自适应方法结合的鲁棒导航算法，通过动态调整测量协方差矩阵来应对传感器中断和振动干扰，在Salin247自主机器人上验证，显著降低3D位置RMSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业自主机器人在非道路环境中常受传感器中断（GNSS/LiDAR/视觉）和高频振动影响，导致状态估计精度下降，现有标准EKF假设恒定测量噪声无法应对突变干扰。

Method: 基于加加速度增强EKF（Jerk-augmented EKF）结合多调谐因子自适应方法（MTF），实时动态调整测量协方差矩阵以适应传感器异常和突变扰动。

Result: 在Salin247农业机器人真实数据上，相比基线EKF模型，3D位置RMSE显著降低，航位推算能力提升。

Conclusion: 加加速度增强结合MTF自适应能有效提升农业机器人在恶劣环境下的导航鲁棒性，提供更可靠的航位推算性能。

Abstract: Precise state estimation for navigation of autonomous agricultural robots is often compromised by sensor outages (GNSS/LiDAR/Visual) and high-frequency vibrations inherent in off-road environments. This paper proposes a robust navigation algorithm based on a jerk-augmented Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) integrated with a Multiple Tuning Factor (MTF) adaptation method. Unlike standard EKF approaches that assume constant measurement noise, our method dynamically adjusts the measurement covariance matrix in real-time, allowing the system to cope with sudden disturbances and sensor outliers. We evaluate the algorithm using real-world data from a Salin247 autonomous robot. Results demonstrate that jerk-augmentation combined with MTF adaptation significantly reduces 3D position Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) compared to baseline EKF models, providing superior dead-reckoning capabilities.

</details>


### [142] [Language-Driven Cost Optimization for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10974)
*Diego Martinez-Baselga,Khaled Mustafa,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个基于大语言模型的驾驶成本函数自适应调参框架，用户可用自然语言描述偏好，LLM生成MPPI控制器的参数，并通过人在环路验证和迭代反馈调整自动驾驶行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶运动规划器的成本函数参数调优需要专业技术知识，难以适应不同交通场景和用户偏好，缺乏直观的人机交互方式。

Method: 利用LLM解析结构化场景描述和自然语言用户查询，生成风险感知MPPI控制器的成本函数参数；引入人在环路验证环节，将提议的行为变化用非技术语言描述并确认；支持部署前后用户反馈，实现迭代优化。

Result: 在多个真实驾驶场景的仿真中，该方法能够以直观的方式诱导出与用户意图一致的行为变化。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效弥合了智能车辆控制系统与终端用户之间的鸿沟，使非技术用户能够通过自然语言定制自动驾驶行为。

Abstract: The driving behavior of autonomous vehicles is typically governed by the cost function of their motion planner, which encodes objectives such as speed tracking, smoothness, lane keeping, and collision avoidance. However, tuning the parameters that shape this cost function is a challenging task that requires technical expertise, limiting the vehicle's ability to adapt to evolving traffic scenarios or end-user preferences. This work presents a language-driven framework for adaptive cost design in autonomous driving. A Large Language Model (LLM) interprets structured scenario descriptions and natural language user queries to generate the parameters applied to a risk-aware Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller. The system incorporates a human-in-the-loop validation stage in which the proposed behavioral changes are described in non-technical language and confirmed prior to deployment. Users may additionally provide feedback either before or after deployment, enabling iterative refinement of the vehicle's motion behavior. The framework is evaluated across multiple queries in realistic driving scenarios to assess its effectiveness. Simulation results demonstrate that the method successfully induces behavioral changes that align with the intended requirements in an intuitive manner, thereby bridging the gap between intelligent vehicle control systems and end users.

</details>


### [143] [Multi-UAV Active Sensing with Information Gain-based Planning and Belief Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10986)
*S. Habibi,L. Marques*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并真实世界验证了一种多无人机主动感知框架，用于概率二值地形图绘制，以精准农业为例，采用因子图建模空间依赖、信息增益驱动路径规划（IGbIPP），并与随机游走和扫描覆盖基线对比。结果表明IGbIPP能更有效降低熵和映射误差，并分析了空间权重、融合规则的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在空间分布环境中进行主动感知时面临飞行时间有限、感知不确定性以及空间覆盖与观测精度之间的权衡，现有方法缺乏真实世界验证，该工作旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用因子图建模地形空间相关性，使用信息增益指导的路径规划（IGbIPP），并对比随机游走和扫描覆盖两种基线；评估多种空间相关权重（等权、偏置权、自适应权）和概率融合规则（贝叶斯、对数几率、Dempster-Shafer），在合成地形和真实无人机农业影像上进行验证。

Result: IGbIPP在熵降低和映射误差方面优于基线；更宽的视场角提升真实覆盖和精度；等权或偏置空间权比重自适应权重更鲁棒；贝叶斯、对数几率、Dempster-Shafer融合实现最佳协作映射性能。

Conclusion: 不确定性驱动的规划、感知几何、空间建模和概率融合对真实世界的无人机主动感知至关重要，为多无人机协同环境感知提供了实用性结论。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly used for active sensing and information gathering in spatially distributed environments. Their performance, however, is constrained by limited flight time, sensing uncertainty, and the trade-off between spatial coverage and observation accuracy. This paper presents a real-world validation of a multi-UAV active sensing framework for probabilistic binary terrain mapping, with precision agriculture used as the application case. The environment is represented as a probabilistic belief map, where spatial dependencies are modeled through a factor-graph formulation. UAV decision making is guided by Information Gain based Informative Path Planning (IGbIPP), and the approach is compared with Random Walk and Sweep coverage path planning baselines using both synthetic terrains and real UAV-derived agricultural imagery. The study also evaluates spatial correlation weights and several probabilistic belief-fusion rules for multi-UAV information sharing. Results show that IGbIPP reduces entropy and mapping error more effectively than the baselines, while a wider field of view improves real-world coverage and map accuracy. The results further show that simple equal or biased spatial weights can be more robust than adaptive weights, and that Bayesian, log-odds, and Dempster--Shafer fusion achieve the best cooperative mapping performance. These findings highlight the importance of uncertainty-driven planning, sensing geometry, spatial modeling, and probabilistic fusion for real-world UAV-based active sensing.

</details>


### [144] [Diffusion Forcing Planner: History-Annealed Planning with Time-Dependent Guidance for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11019)
*Zehan Zhang,Neng Zhang,Yaoyi Li,Jia Cai,Zhiling Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出扩散强制规划器（DFP），通过将轨迹分解为历史、当前和未来段并分配独立噪声等级，在联合去噪过程中利用历史引导控制，实现连续、稳定、可控的端到端运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的运动规划器存在时间不一致性问题，小扰动跨帧累积导致闭环驾驶中轨迹不稳定；注入历史作为静态条件信号会导致规划器复制历史模式而非适应环境上下文。

Method: 将完整轨迹分解为历史段、当前段和未来段，为各段分配独立噪声等级；执行异质联合扩散过程同时去噪历史和未来段；推理时使用分类器无关引导（CFG）通过退火历史以可控方式引导未来采样。

Result: 在nuPlan上的闭环评估和全面消融实验表明，DFP在复杂驾驶场景中实现了竞争性性能，并产生连续、稳定、可控的运动规划。

Conclusion: DFP通过历史引导的异质联合扩散过程有效解决了学习型运动规划器的时间不一致性问题，兼顾稳定性与环境适应性。

Abstract: Learning-based motion planners, despite recent progress, often suffer from temporal inconsistency. Small perturbations across frames can accumulate into unstable trajectories, degrading comfort and safety in closed-loop driving. Several methods attempt to inject history as a static conditioning signal to stabilize outputs, only to induce the planner to copy historical patterns instead of adapting to environment contexts. To address this limitation, we propose Diffusion Forcing Planner (DFP), a diffusion-based planning framework driven by history-guided control. Specifically, DFP decomposes the full trajectory into history, current and future segments, and assign independent noise levels to each segment. The model jointly denoises the historical and the future segments, enforcing a heterogeneous joint diffusion process. At inference, classifier-free guidance (CFG) is applied to steer future sampling using annealed history in a controllable manner. Closed-loop evaluation and comprehensive ablations on nuPlan show that DFP achieves competitive performance while producing continuous, stable, and controllable motion plans in complex driving scenarios.

</details>


### [145] [A Spiking Neural Architecture for Coordinating Arm and Locomotor Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11034)
*Lea Steffen,Kathryn Simone,Graeme Damberger,Travis DeWolf,Hudson Ly,Chris Eliasmith*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个将双足 locomotion 与手臂控制整合的脉冲神经网络控制器，在模拟人形机器人上实现目标抓取、数字绘制、路径跟踪及行走与手臂控制切换，基于 NEF 和 SPA 框架，通过基底节模型实现高阶动作选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SNN 电机控制系统将双足行走与手臂控制分离处理，缺乏两者在整个人形机器人平台上的联合控制方案。

Method: 基于神经工程框架（NEF）和语义指针架构（SPA）设计脉冲神经网络，采用基底节模型实现行走与手臂控制的高阶动作选择切换，在 Nengo 与 Isaac Sim 联合仿真中验证。

Result: 成功实现目标到达、连续数字绘制、路径跟踪行走，以及通过基底节去抑制实现行走与手臂控制的切换。

Conclusion: 首个将双足行走与手臂控制整合的全尺度人形机器人脉冲神经网络控制器，全脉冲实现可部署于低功耗神经形态硬件。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) coupled with neuromorphic hardware offer energy-efficient solutions for humanoid robot control. However, existing SNN-based motor control systems address bipedal locomotion and arm control in isolation, leaving integrated control of both unaddressed. We present a spiking architecture that coordinates force-based arm control and bipedal locomotion in a simulated humanoid, using the Neural Engineering Framework (NEF) and Semantic Pointer Architecture (SPA). High-level action selection between locomotor and arm control is mediated by a biologically grounded spiking basal ganglia model. We validate the system through co-simulation of Nengo, for the neural control, and Isaac Sim, demonstrating successful target reaching, continuous digit drawing, path-following locomotion, and finally, switching between walking and arm control via basal ganglia disinhibition. To our knowledge, this is the first integrated spiking controller to combine bipedal locomotion and arm control on a full-scale humanoid platform. The full spike-based implementation enables future deployment on low-power neuromorphic hardware.

</details>


### [146] [Generation of Diverse and Functional Robot Designs using Superquadrics Parametrisation and Quality-Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11037)
*Leni Le Goff,Simon Smith,Emma Hart*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于超二次曲面（SQs）的机器人形态表示方法，结合质量-多样性算法MAP-Elites进行机器人进化生成设计，相比CPPN和标准EA，在保持形态与功能多样性方面取得更优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统进化算法在机器人生成设计中容易早熟收敛到少量次优设计，缺乏多样性。需要一种既能紧凑表示形态又能有效维持种群多样性的方法。

Method: 采用超二次曲面（SQs）作为机器人身体的几何表示，SQs是可解释、紧凑且计算高效的3D形状数学表示；将其与质量-多样性算法MAP-Elites结合，对比CPPN表示和标准EA在不同环境下的表现。

Result: SQ+MAP-Elites组合在两个测试环境中均取得最高的QD-score，在多样性和功能性设计方面均优于其他组合。

Conclusion: 紧凑可解释的几何表示（SQs）结合显式多样性机制（MAP-Elites）能有效提升机器人进化设计中生成方案的质量和数量。

Abstract: Generative design of robots requires navigating a vast search-space, encompassing physical configurations and behavioural parameters. Evolutionary Algorithms (EAs) have shown promising results, but often converge prematurely to a small set of sub-optimal designs. Most EAs fail to maintain sufficient diversity in the population that would allow the discovery of distinct functional robots. To counter premature convergence, we introduce a superquadrics-based representation (SQs) for robot bodies. SQs are interpretable, compact and computationally efficient mathematical representations of 3D geometrical shapes that can be tuned to specific design-spaces. To encourage morphological diversity, we combine this representation with a quality-diversity (QD) algorithm (MAP-Elites). We compare SQs and Compositional Pattern Producing Networks representations as generators of morphologies, combining them with standard EAs and MAP-Elites.
